金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元 ； 公司同時將2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%， 反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險 ， 油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力 ：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施 ，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變 ， 但短線波動勢將加劇 ：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫 、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期 ， 可見AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象 ，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性 ， 加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加 、 期限溢酬及利率波動影響 ， 因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

26Q4結論-上游材料加速更新，漲價仍為主旋律
1.PCB上游:供不應求推動漲價動力，新材料應用下帶來新機會
• 玻纖布:E-Glass(高階)/LowDK-2/T-Glass 在2026Q4~2027年呈現全面緊缺，成為推動當前上游材料漲價的主要
因素，也是決定結構性缺貨的關鍵原料，玻布廠迎來產品組合改善+報價上揚雙重催化。
• 銅箔:HVLP4加速驗證日台以外的供應商，中系擴產積極+中系設備加速採用下，維持2027年供需缺口放緩預期。
• 銅箔基板:PTFE樹脂為美系客戶解決互聯的確定性解方，成為銅箔基板材料中新晉路線；傳統的PPO/CH樹脂仍
有一片天，26Q4 VR300 設計將定案，PTFE+M8/M9設計呼聲較高。
• 鑽針:高階鍍膜針需求攀升，供應商在2026~2028年加快投入建置產能，新材料(PTFE)仍有一定的鑽針消耗，產
品組合的改變遠比漲價來的重要。
2. IC載板: 產能全數售罄，缺料與缺產能並行，關注供應商漲價態度。
• 供需缺口:維持2026/2027年 20%+/40%+ 缺口預期。

• 主邏輯為面積放大+層數增加，大廠產能售罄，客戶需要付出更高的價格預定產能+料源。
3. PCB:關注AWS、Google新品放量，以及2027~2028年HDI設計的變化，聚焦Tier1供應體系和伺服器
級HDI稀缺產能。
4. 建議首選關注個股:
• 高階稀缺HDI產能:2368金像電、3037欣興、4958臻鼎
• 產品組合優化顯著:1303南亞、1815富喬、8021尖點、8039台虹
• 缺貨27~28年不緩解+AI Agent受惠:8046南電、3189景碩、3037欣興、4958 臻鼎

表1:OBBA法案和AI行動計畫
全年核心:政策主導AI為基本方針
法案 大而美法案(One Big Beautiful Bill Act) AI行動計劃(America's AI Action Plan)
內容 1. 下調聯邦所得稅:從35%下調至21%，
減輕企業稅賦負擔。
2. 恢復2017年減稅與就業法案項目中
允許企業當年資本支出可以全額抵
減稅賦(折舊扣除)，有利重資本支出
廠商加快投資回收時間，受惠廠商
如CSP大廠。
3. 國內R&D全額費用化: 為了獎勵企業
在美國進行生產研發，符合條件的
R&D費用可以於當年度全面費用化，
受惠廠商如Open AI、Anthropic等。
1. 加速AI創新:確保美國擁有全球最強
大的AI系統，放鬆相關管制與障礙，
並鼓勵開源等。
2. 增加AI基礎設施建置:提升美國AI算
力基礎設施硬體基礎，以滿足AI推
動經濟和安全的需求；加快關鍵項
目的審核，加快建設資料中心、晶
圓代工廠、電力基礎設施，掃除一

切AI基礎設施擴充的障礙。
3. 維護國際外交與AI安全:在全球AI競
爭中掌握規則制定權，輸出美國AI
技術，同時阻止對手利用美國創新
超越美國，嚴防高階算力流向競爭
國家，向盟友出口美國AI算力技術
等，有利主權AI(Sovereign AI)的發
展。。
資料來源:福邦投顧整理
• AI 成為美國政策上的基本方針，OBBBA法案刺激企業加大投資AI算力，AAAP計畫掃除建
置AI算力的瓶頸和主權AI方針的確立。

鑽針主邏輯—PCB廠積極擴產
• PCB設計日益複雜，耗用大量產能：過去伺服器板層數約16~2X層，目前主流已經來到
20~30層，未來30~50層越顯常見。
• PCB用量增加，耗用大量產能:隨著各家設計陸續參考NVIDIA Rack設計，除了原有的主
板(GPU/ASIC 使用)，亦新增更多副板，例如Switchboard、midplane等，終端客戶積極
驗證新供應廠商。

### Page 12

12【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
PCB鑽針需求呈現翻倍成長-CCL材料影響
◼ PCB用鑽針受到板材影響，在同樣板厚下，每針可鑽孔次數大幅縮水:
• M6~M7時主針鑽孔壽命約600~800次
• M8材料下可鑽400~500次
• M9材料下僅剩下100~200次
• 材料越高階，鑽針使用量會是過往的6倍以上。
過去 目前 未來
對應材質 M6~M7 M8/(有布/無布+球型矽粉) M9~M10
主針壽命 1000/600~800次 400~500次 100~200次
表5:鑽針基於不同板材的使用壽命&各應用對應的鑽針方案
資料來源:產業調研、福邦投顧整理預估

### Page 13

13【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
PCB鑽針需求呈現翻倍成長-長徑比帶動報價上揚
• 當前主流GPU用PCB約5mm厚度，隨著AI用PCB板厚的提升，每增加1mm，製造上可能會採
用增加一根預鑽針，採用預鑽針預鑽至特定深度，後續用主針完成加工。
• GPU伺服器:GB200採用「1主針配2白針」→GB300採用「1主針配3白針」→VR200採用「
1主針配4白針」，
• AI用的鑽針，長度/直徑比超過30規格的鑽針，報價上會採用一個方案一個價格，因為
加工精度要求提高，難度變大；目前AI用的鍍膜鑽針報價較傳統白針多20~50%，若是
這種報價方式，特別是板厚超過7mm，產品售價將會多40~50%；未來約在2027~2028年
時，7.5~8.5mm的鑽針將成為主流，報價將會是一般白針的6倍以上報價。
• 毛利率方向:AI用的鍍膜鑽針鑽針，根據產業訪查，產品毛利率約50%+。
GPU Server ASIC Server 交換機
對應鑽針方案 1主針
+1~4根預鑽白針
2主針
+1根預鑽白針

擠效應，白針類產品外溢給凱崴等廠商。

### Page 20

20【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
• 下個世代的半導體封裝需要更大的面積來放置更多元件、支持2D~3D封裝(CoWoS~SOIC)
，因此材料的要求需要Low CTE、高彈性變量來達到低翹曲度（Warpage)和連接可靠度
。
• 根據Resonac白皮書，為了解決內埋元件載板對於核心層的厚度精度要求，較薄的核心
層或不均勻的厚度，會導致內埋元件的損壞，因此之後許多載板核心的設計會採用厚
Core+ low CTE 膠片（採用T/Q Glass)。
• 2026年的TPU，2027年的Maia300都有見到這類核心Core CCL+PP的設計方案
(Multilayer Core)，此也會多消耗T-Glass的供應量。
T-Glass:客戶積極導入抗翹曲設計
圖5:Resonac Type F產品

### Page 30

30【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
無布VS.有布方向選擇
⚫ PTFE 由於Dk/Df的性能優於電子布，如果PTFE搭配電子布，會導致其Dk/Df轉差。
⚫ 未來走向:
1. 有布方案(M9~M10搭配LowDK2~4電子布):若PCB上須放置CPU/GPU/ASIC等晶片，考量尺寸
安定性因素，會需要搭配具有電子布的銅箔基板，例如UBB、ComputeBoard、
SwitchBoard、MotherBoard。
2. 無布方案(搭配無布膠片):若僅考連傳輸性能(不放置晶片)，目前採取的無布方案，樹
脂填矽粉/球型矽微粉的方案為主流，以改善膨脹性。

主要以球型粉料為主。

### Page 32

32【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
PCB製造端的製程變化-銅漿燒結
⚫ 銅漿燒結製程源於瞬態液相燒結(TLPS)技術，主要用於實現PCB盲/埋孔電路層間的Z軸
互連。
⚫ 過去傳統高多層多採用背鑽的製程，但在NV陣營，開始嘗試使用”銅漿燒結“的製程來
處理高多層版的訊號連接，以解決背鑽精度控制的問題。
⚫ 目前具有【燒結製程+大板製造能力】的主要為板廠，例如深南電路、景旺電子、勝宏
科技、滬電股份、生益電子；台系則有金像電具備此製程能力。
圖10:PCB銅燒結概念示意圖
資料來源:產業調研、福邦投顧整理

OpenClaw:加速從LLM往AI Agent 發展
◼ OpenClaw:開放式AI助理項目，可以根據使用者指令在應用程式和線上服務之間自行處
理複雜任務，並被設計為可以代替使用者執行任務的AI軟體，並非過往的聊天機器人。
◼ OpenAI推出GPT5.4，此次更新提供Thinking和Pro兩種模型； Pro主要適用於需要進行
複雜任務的使用者，Thinking模型可以在產出答案前提出思考計畫，使用者可以從中調
整任務方向→強化Agent能力，可以支持智慧裝置執行複雜的工作流程。
◼ NVIDIA將推出類似OpenClaw的開放式平台-NemoClaw。

### Page 36

39【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Agentic AI的瓶頸主要來自CPU
◼ 根據論文《A CPU-Centric Perspective on Agentic AI》一文指出，可以看出執行時間中
CPU延遲占了大比例（紅圈），最高可達到90%以上；吞吐量瓶頸除了來自GPU，亦會
來自CPU;能耗方面，大批次處理中(Batch size提升至128)，CPU平行處理效率低於GPU
，佔到總能耗超過44%，延遲會從2.8秒提升至6秒以上，超過2倍。
資料來源: 《A CPU-Centric Perspective on Agentic AI》 、福邦投顧整理
說明:Haystack RAG（問答檢索）/Toolformer（數學運算與 API 呼叫）/ChemCrow（化學文獻檢索）
Langchain（網路搜尋與摘要）/SWE-Agent（程式碼與 Bash/Python 執行)
圖11:Agent下，CPU佔據了大部分的延遲

### Page 37

40【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
CPU朝向多核發展→推升載板面積
◼ Agent執行:API處理、資料解析、腳本執行，90%由CPU完成，為了降低延遲，CPU往多
核心發展是趨勢。
◼ 記憶體與上下文切換:CPU需要頻繁在不同Agent狀態做切換，多核心架構能讓每個
Agent有獨立的處理單元，降低上下文切換帶來的效率損耗。
◼ 沙盒隔離/安全:Agent 需要大量獨立的一般伺服器建立Sandbox來執行，使用量與使用
者和使用量正相關，需要更多核心數、高頻寬。
資料來源:Intel、AMD、福邦投顧整理預估

傳統光通訊模組板-HDI/mSAP供應體系的新機會
◼因應高度整合和小型化需求，光模組開始大量採用MSAP來縮小產品體積和損耗；進入
1.6T，L/S 要求微縮至20µm-25µm；因此1.6T開始，強制採用MSAP。
◼材料規格上，亦往M7~M8等級以上升級，但在MSAP部分，會大量採用載體銅箔(三井金屬
90%市佔率)、日東紡的Low CTE布，目前有面臨缺料的情況。

交換機板:主流AI層數較低，傳統乙太網多層為主
◼ 資料中心需要承載流量大+高速傳輸，在此需求下，高多層板成為此需求的解決方案，
其優點在於電子元件間連線縮短下，訊號傳輸速度提高，而且也方遍佈線。
◼乙太網交換機為各家CSP+主權主要Scale out的主流，800G報價是400G的2倍以上，1.6T
是800G的4倍以上。

### Page 1

Lotes

Lighter 3Q26 sales +4% QoQ; improving 
margins; reiterate Buy, PO to NT$2,245

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 2,245 TWD | Price: 1,915 TWD

Sep sales +5% MoM; lighter 3Q26 sales at +4% QoQ  
Lotes posted Sep sales of NT$3.4bn (+5% MoM, +16% YoY). 3Q26 sales of NT$9.9bn 
(+4% QoQ, +18% YoY) were 11%/3% below BofAe/consensus. We attributed the lighter 
sales mainly to industry-wise key component shortage (e.g. memory, CPU, PCB), which 
likely capped the demand in general server and PC. While sales mix by segment is yet to 
be disclosed, we believe server should remain the biggest segment (BofAe: 55%) in 
3Q26, vs. 54%/40% in 2Q26/3Q25. We now project 3Q26 GM at 45.7%, similar to 
45.5% in 2Q26, vs. 51.2% in 3Q25, mainly due to higher raw material cost. 
Gradually improving margins; new platform from ’27 
We project 4Q26 sales +4% QoQ (+12% YoY) amid likely fewer working days in Oct/Dec 
and market demand push out due to component shortage. Yet, we believe Lotes’ 
quarterly GM should gradually improve with new CPU platform launches in early 2027 
and more new products. We project 2027/28E GM at 49.0%/49.9%, considering 
potential margin dilution from QD (BofAe: 1.4%/3.5%/5.0% of 2026/27/28E sales). 
PO to NT$2,245; reiterate Buy; cost pressure fully aware  
We cut 2026-28E EPS by 9-18% to reflect 3Q26 preliminary sales, more conservative 
margin assumptions under material cost pressure, and higher share count (+1.3%) for 
new CB (total NT$300mn) and fund-raising plan (maximum 1.3mn shares). We cut PO to 
NT$2,245 (20x 2027E EPS) from NT$2,800 (was 22x) with valuation base unchanged but 
a lower P/E multiple. We lower P/E multiple to 20x (2SD above historical average) on 
industry-wise component tightness, which may partially cap CPU server market demand. 
In our view, Lotes’ near-term margin pressure has been fully reflected as its share price 
corrected 15% in 3Q26, vs. TAIEX +4%. We reiterate our Buy rating on Lotes as a clear 
CPU server beneficiary to capture still robust market demand (see Vivek Arya’s 15 Sep 
note), with attractive valuation under 24%/36% sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28.

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 7.

Company Sector 
Connectors, Passive Components & Distribution

Company Description 
Lotes was founded in Keelung, Taiwan, in 1986, and went 
publicly listed on Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2007. Lotes is 
the global top-tier PC/server CPU socket supplier. It also 
supplies connector/cable/harness for PC, server, auto and 
industrial. Key end-customers include global PC brands 
(Lenovo, HP, Dell, Apple) and global datacenter hyperscalers 
(FAMG). Besides headquarter in Taiwan, Lotes has 
production sites in China (Guangzhou, Suzhou, and Hunan) 
and Vietnam.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Lotes in view of (1) solid CPU 
server demand outlook, (2) ongoing server/DT CPU platform 
upgrade to support ASP, (3) double-digit sales/earnings 
CAGR in 2026-28 with margin improvement in 2027-28, (4) 
solid balance sheet, and (5) attractive valuation. AI business 
remains the watch point with potential upside.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Lotes (ZYZTF) 
Our PO of NT$2,245 is based on 20x 2027E EPS. Our valuation multiple of 20x (2SD 
above historical P/E trading average) reflects rising sales mix from better-margin server 
business with ASP increase, double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28, and solid balance 
sheet. Our P/E multiple can also be justified by solid CPU server demand amid 
accelerating agentic AI needs, Lotes' top position as desktop/server CPU socket supplier 
to ride on Intel/AMD's ongoing desktop/server CPU platform upgrade.

Upside risks: (1) further ASP increase thanks to faster adoption/penetration of new 
DT/server platform and new product, (2) faster progress in AI related business, (3) faster 
AI PC launch/adoption, (4) GM upside from lowering/stabilizing raw material prices, and 
(5) more favorable FX (USD/NTD and/or USD/RMB) movement to benefit GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks: (1) slower ASP increase due to new CPU platform delay, (2) slower 
progress from AI business, (3) weaker PC/server market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (4) GM pressure from higher raw material prices, (5) higher labor/logistics 
cost from supply-chain disruption and uncertain China lockdown policy, (6) unfavorable 
FX movement to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items, and (7) peers' more 
aggressive pricing competition.

Update
Alchip Technologies Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Solid 2027 and 2028 outlook
with potential catalysts; OW
What's new? Ahead of Alchip's 3Q26 earnings release on November 6, investors are
looking for updates on the company's 2027 and 2028 revenue outlook, particularly
regarding AWS Trainium programmes and potential new customer wins.
Major projects – AWS Trainium: It appears TSMC is allocating additional 3nm
capacity to AI ASICs and CPUs in 2027. We do not rule out AWS-related revenue

growing more than 70% YoY in 2027, versus our current forecast of 56% YoY
growth. As a result, we remain confident in our 2027 revenue forecast of US$4.4bn.
We believe gross margin can remain sustainable despite the additional revenue
contribution, which would be better than our previous assumption of fixed gross
profit from 3nm Trainium3. For 2028, we forecast a Street-high US$8bn revenue
contribution from 2nm Trainium4. Our supply-chain checks suggest US$8bn–12bn of

revenue is possible if AWS allocates additional volume.
Potential new projects - Google KGD and others:  In  Asia-Pacific Technology: AI
Supply Chain: Google ASIC, who gets what for design services? (24 Aug 2026), we
highlighted key trends for Google TPU v10, including: "KGD (known good die) for the
compute die, which means Google owns the compute-die design and photomask
tools, while retaining flexibility to outsource backend design services for the

compute die." We believe Alchip is a leading candidate for this KGD backend-design
opportunity. In addition, Alchip is pursuing another US HPC customer opportunity.
Our checks suggest a switch-networking product is a possibility, potentially
involving collaboration with Astera Labs and NVLink Fusion.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 3

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Rather than now slowing down, it’s actually getting stronger
TSMC reported an upbeat 3Q26 result with robust 4Q26 outlook. It guided for
4Q26 revenue of US$52.3–54.3 billion, representing about 14% QoQ growth and
58% year-over-year growth at the midpoint (based on FX at NT$31.8 per USD).
4Q26 GM/OPM is expected at 65–67% and 56.5–58.5%. TSMC reiterated its 2026
capex to be US$60–64 billion and said investment from 2026 to 2028 will be

significantly higher than in the previous three years, reflecting strong multi-year
customer demand, including agentic AI. It is also worth noting that TSMC plans to
begin using the High-NA EUV technology around 2030 and develop a 12-inch
mask capability by 2031, when it expects the tools to offer sufficient maturity and
cost effectiveness. Management said this timing does not undermine its
confidence in technology leadership.
Management described AI-related demand as exceptionally strong, supported by

accelerators, networking chips, and rising demand for CPUs associated with
agentic AI. TSMC’s previously guided AI GPU/accelerator CAGR to be mid-to-high
50%, but now sees more upside ahead. Given rising concern on AI expanding
peaking out, the management said it is clear that its capacity planning strategy has
considered customer forecasts, data-center construction, power availability, and
other supply-chain constraints. Management still judges demand to be above

available supply and is working to close the gap, while emphasizing disciplined
planning to limit the risk of overbuilding.
Figure 1. TSMC – 3Q26 results comparison
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Citi Research, Bloomberg
Capacity expansion plan and allocation
During the earnings call, TSMC also mentioned that despite the tight capacity
supply, customers cannot generally sublet or auction wafer capacity, while there is

limited case that collaboration is through mask-license agreements. For the
advanced packaging, TSMC has announced its Global Foundries and VIS’
agreement on interposer as part of its backend supply strategy, intended to help
ensure customers have enough capacity to bring products to market; It also
reiterated that TSMC’s CoWoS remains as the leading customer’s choice and is
already in volume production at 5x reticle size, while it is developing 9x and larger

solutions and evaluating CoPoS.

### Page 4

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Reiterate Buy, best position in AI arena
Our earnings projection is largely unchanged for 2027 /2028 earnings. While its
capex expansion, overseas operation and N2 will likely be a margin dilution factor
initially, due to its cross-node leverage, high UTR and yield rate, we believe its GM
downside is manageable. Overall, we see strong advanced node demand into next
year and the long-term order visibility will be supported for higher capex cycle.  We

maintain our TP to NT$4,000, based on unchanged 25x of 2027 EPS estimate;
reiterate Buy.
Key TSMC earnings call recap
n4Q26 guidance
–Sales: US$52.3-54.3bn (+14% QoQ at the midpoint)
–GPM: 65-67% (assuming USDTWD=31.8)
–OPM: 56.5-58.5%
n2026 full-year & long-term guidance
–Full year sales: raise 2026 full-year USD-based sales guidance from more
than 40% growth to +44% growth
–Full year capex: retain its 2026 capex guidance at US$60-64bn (70-80%
advanced process, 10% specialty, 10-20% for advanced packaging, testing

and masks)
–GM outlook
•4Q GM to be impacted by N2 and overseas fab ramps, but partially
supported by productivity improvement, UTR enhancement and
optimizing capacity allocation
•GM dilution from overseas fab would be c2ppts impact for the full-year
2026; with more new capacity expansion in the US, the margin dilution
impact would widen to 3-4ppts in the next few years.
–Long-term outlook
•Retains 5-year AI sales CAGR (2024-2029) remains at high-50% or above

nCapacity planning
–TSMC currently has 20 fabs in total. It plans to add 13 fabs in Taiwan and add
5-6 overseas fabs.
–N2 capacity will double in 2027; it has also started preparing for A14 capacity
with volume production starting from 2028.
–US Arizona fab 2 construction is complete and begin volume production in
3Q27, ahead of schedule.
–JP fab 2 would produce N3 with mass production from 2028

### Page 10

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
10
TSMC
Company description
TSMC is the founder and leader of the dedicated IC foundry segment. The
company has built its reputation by offering advanced and "more-than-
Moore" wafer production processes and industry-leading manufacturing
efficiency. From its inception in 1987, TSMC has consistently offered the
foundry segment's leading technologies and TSMC-compatible design
services.
Investment strategy

We rate TSMC shares as Buy. We expect TSMC to deliver sales growth of
>40% in recent years thanks to strong growth from HPC/AI as well as stable
demand growth from smartphone, Io T, and automotive on tech migration and
a shortened replacment cycle. CPU outsourcing to TSMC offers further
growth potential. A rising cash dividend should also support the share price.
While TSMC has been increasing its capex meaningfully since 2009, its cost
structure remaisn competitive through continuous product mix

improvements, efficiency enhancements, and cost reductions.
Valuation
Our target price for TSMC shares of NT$4,000 is based on 25x our 2027E
EPS, higher than its three-year average forward PER, versus global peers’
average of 16-25x. We believe our target PER is justified by TSMC's leading
position in advanced process node and robust AI demand growth outlook.
Given its continuing leadership in the semiconductor foundry industry, we
believe TSMC’s stable order visibility and earnings outlook make it less

vulnerable to a potential global economic downturn than some of its peers.
Our target price equates to 2026E/27E P/E of ~ 36x/25x and 2026E/27E P/B of
~13x/10x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from achieving our target
price include: 1) weakness in the global semiconductor market; 2) larger-
than-expected margin contractions due to depreciation cost increases and
strong NTD; 3) competitors entering the foundry business; 4) longer-than-

expected digestion of inventory in the supply chain; and 5) a slowdown in
demand either due to a global economic downturn or a global trade
disruption triggered by tariffs.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com 
       廖婉婷 TINA_LIAO@uni-psg.com

臻鼎 9 月營收 263.58 億元 ， 改寫單月新高，3Q26 營收 646.24 
億元，優於研究部原估的 607.09 億元，主因 AI 相關業務出貨
優於預期 ， 加上手機客戶進入新品旺季 ， 研究部據此上修 3Q26 稅
後 EPS 至 3.84 元（原估 3.47 元）。展望 4Q26，行動通訊旺
季延續， 加上 GPU 與 ASIC 新一代主板開始放量 ，營收與獲利將
續創新高 。2027 年伺服器與光模塊隨新平台出貨擴大及 1.6T 成
為主流， 營收可望各再增逾百億元；IC 載板亦受惠供給持續吃緊
及新產能陸續開出 ， 帶動 AI 相關營收占比突破三成 。 綜上所述 ，
研究部上修臻鼎 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 12.11 元（+99.6% 
YoY，原估 11.81 元）及 26.14 元（+118.6% YoY，原估 22.91 
元）。基於臻鼎產品橫跨軟板、類載板、HDI、HLC 與 IC 載板，
可為客戶提供一站式解決方案 ， 並提前掌握關鍵材料與設備產能 ，
在高階 PCB 供給吃緊下擴產得以大量開出搶奪市占，研究部維
持以 2027 年獲利為評價基礎及 28 倍本益比，目標價上調至 
730 元，維持買進投資評等。

重點摘要： 
1. 9 月營收改寫新高，上修 2H26 獲利預估 
臻鼎 9 月營收 263.58 億元（+27.5% MoM、+36.1% YoY），改寫
單月新高 ， 其中伺服器 、 光模塊與 IC 載板合計營收年增逾三倍 ，
行動通訊亦呈雙位數月增 與年增 。3Q26 營收 646.24 億元
（+33.4% QoQ、+36.4% YoY），達研究部原估的 106.4%，明顯優
於預期 ， 主因 GPU 新一代主板進入量產 、1.6T 光模塊出貨放大 ，
加上手機客戶進入新品旺季。考量 AI 相關產品占比提升，研究
部預估 3Q26 毛利率為 25.5%（+2.4ppt QoQ、+3.5ppt YoY，原
估 24.7%），稅後 EPS 上修至 3.84 元（+81.9% QoQ 、+81.9% 
YoY，原估 3.47 元） 。展望 4Q26，行動通訊旺季延續，加上 GPU 
與 ASIC 新一代主板放量，研究部預估 4Q26 營收 736.13 億元
（+13.9% QoQ、+29.4% YoY），稅後 EPS 4.75 元（+23.8% QoQ、
+70.4% YoY，原估 4.86 元），營收與獲利續創新高。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. AI 伺服器主板成為最主要成長動能 
AI 伺服器主板隨 GPU 與 ASIC 平台升級，晶片封裝尺寸、I/O 
數與傳輸速率同步提升，主板須在有限面積內完成更多高速訊號
的扇出與繞線。 傳統 HLC 以一次壓合搭配機械鑽孔與背鑽 ，層間
連接密度已近極限；高階 HDI 則以多次壓合與雷射盲孔逐層堆
疊，線路更細、訊號路徑更短，可降低高速傳輸損耗，帶動 GPU 
平台 OAM 等主板設計由 HLC 轉向 HDI。HDI 須投入大量雷射鑽
孔機與多次壓合產能，在設備與材料交期拉長下，全球高階  AI 
PCB 產能缺口約 15–20%， 訂單已排至 2028 年，2027–2028 年
產品報價亦將持續上調。臻鼎提早取得全球逾五成高階雷射鑽孔
機產能 ， 在建 22 座新廠中半數投入 HDI、 伺服器與光模塊產品 ，
在同業擴產受限下得以持續承接訂單，帶動伺服器 HDI 比重由 
1H26 的約五成於 2H26 明顯提升；繼 ASIC 專案今年大量出貨
後，GPU 平台新一代主板亦自 4Q26 起放量。研究部預估臻鼎伺
服器營收 2026、2027 年各增加逾 100 億元 ， 為未來兩年最主要
成長動能。

3. 臻鼎可望躍居 1.6T 光模塊 PCB 龍頭 
AI 伺服器傳輸速率由 800G 升級至 1.6T，單通道速率倍增使線
路須更細 、 阻抗控制更精準 ， 傳統減成法已難以達到 ， 光模塊 PCB 
因此改採 mSAP 製程。臻鼎過去為手機類載板主要供應商，具備 
mSAP 大量生產經驗 ； 在高階設備交期拉長 、 關鍵材料供給吃緊下 ，
臻鼎已掌握市場上多數高階設備產能並持續擴充，同時提前鎖定
上游材料，得以在淮安與泰國同步擴充光模塊產能，研究部推估
臻鼎可望在 2027 年 1.6T 世代成為全球光模塊 PCB 龍頭。隨 
1.6T 出貨占比由 2026 年的約五成提升至 2027 年逾七成，研
究部預估臻鼎光模塊營收由 2025 年約 9 億元躍升至 2026 年
逾 100 億元，2027 年再增至逾 250 億元。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

4. ABF 與 BT 同步吃緊，新產能與轉單推升載板營收 
AI 晶片封裝面積與層數持續放大，ABF 載板用量與製程難度同
步提升，惟新廠建置期長、上游材料吃緊，供需缺口持續擴大；
同業亦將部分 BT 產能轉往 ABF，使 BT 載板同步出現缺貨，兩
者報價自下半年起持續上漲。臻鼎 ABF 載板技術規格已達最大 
158×158mm、 最高 28 層 ， 深圳一廠 1Q26 轉盈並將於年底滿載 ，
二廠 1Q27 開始貢獻，第二園區 2028 年起約每年開出一座新
廠，且新產能幾乎全由國際客戶預先包下，高雄廠亦已量產先進
封裝相關載板。BT 載板雖無新建廠計畫， 但近期承接同業轉單並
調漲報價 ， 加上產品與客戶組合持續優化 ， 可望維持雙位數成長 。
隨 ABF 產能接續開出，研究部預估 ABF 占載板營收比重將由 
2025 年的 35% 提升至 2027 年約七成，IC 載板營收 2026、
2027 年分別成長 99.2% 及 89.0%。

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

5. 高階新產能陸續開出，產品組合優化推升毛利率 
臻鼎 2026 年資本支出逾 800 億元，2027 年維持相當水準，廠
房數將由目前的 28 座擴增至 2030 年底的 59 座。新產能自 
2027 年起陸續開出：淮安 6 座新廠於 2026 年底完工後進入裝
機，HD 園區預計 3M27 投產、2Q27 大批量產；泰國新廠於 4Q26 
起試產，1H27 進入小量生產 ； 深圳第三園區與載板第二園區則預
計自 2028 年起接棒 （表二） 。新廠初期稼動率與良率仍在爬坡，
加上 2027 年折舊費用預計較 2026 年再增加 20% 至 30%，短
期將稀釋毛利率；惟新增產能以 AI 伺服器、光模塊與 IC 載板
為主，隨 AI 相關營收占比由 2025 年的 11.7% 提升至 2027 
年突破三成，研究部預估臻鼎毛利率將由 2026 年的 24.5% 提
升至 2027 年的 29.3%。

6. 上修 2026、2027 年獲利預估 
2026 年 AI 相關業務為主要成長動能 ，研究部預估伺服器 、光模
塊與其他營收成長 269.0%，IC 載板成長 99.2%，行動通訊受惠
摺疊機新品成長 6.9%，電腦消費則與 2025 年持平，AI 相關營
收占比將由 2025 年的 11.7% 提升至 25.9%。研究部預估 2026 
年營收 2,273.96 億元（+24.6% YoY，原估 2,267.70 億元），
毛利率由 24.1% 上修至 24.5%（+4.7ppt YoY ），稅後 EPS 由 
11.81 元上修至 12.11 元（+99.6% YoY）。 
展望 2027 年，伺服器與光模塊隨新平台出貨擴大及 1.6T 成為
主流，營收合計成長逾一倍，IC 載板受惠 AI 客戶訂單放量、
ABF 比重提升及報價調漲，成長 89.0%，行動通訊與電腦消費則
分別維持中、低個位數成長。在高毛利產品占比提升、費用率隨
營收規模擴大回落，抵銷新廠折舊增加下，研究部預估 2027 年
營收 3,158.99 億元（+38.9% YoY，原估 3,083.20 億元），毛
利率提升至 29.3%（+4.8ppt YoY，原估 28.1%） ，營益率提升至 
17.9%（原估 14.8%） ， 稅後 EPS 上修至 26.14 元（+118.6% YoY，
原估 22.91 元）。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

泰合(6467)

承銷價:105.6 
興櫃價(低/高)： 
32.25/302

Target Price:260

泰合主力產品 BEQIA 專為吞嚥困難族群設計 ， 以Apixaban 為成分
的抗血栓口溶膜，鎖定高齡、吞嚥困難及需長期抗血栓治療族群 。
BEQIA 於 2026 年 6 月 22 日重新送件美國 FDA，進入 10 個月
PDUFA 審查期，2027 年 4 月 22 日為審核完成目標日期，可望於
2Q27 取得美國暫時性核准(Tentative Approval)。 歐洲EMA 方面 ，
則於 2026 年 7 月 17 日完成 Day 121 關鍵清單回覆 ， 朝CHMP 最
終審查意見(Day 210)與 EC 核准推進，公司預計 4Q26 可望獲歐盟
上市許可 。授權策略方面，泰合採取原廠與非原廠雙軌並行 。 對原廠
而言，BEQIA 可借力其成熟通路快速推升口溶模市占，並在面臨專
利懸崖與學名藥競爭時，可藉此維持利潤表現，洽談時即以解決原
廠現有困境的定位切入 。 至於非原廠 ， 泰合採精準區域分擊 ， 鎖定主
要市場以外的區域優先展開談判，累積市場實績與聲量。 泰合目前
已在歐美、紐澳、拉丁美洲等地區與數十家藥廠簽訂NDA，並持續
洽談，多家跨國藥廠與各區域BD 代表已進入實質洽談階段。此外，
下一代產品 TAH3341 長效口溶膜雖仍處臨床前，但也有廠商表達
興趣並關注發展進度 。泰合對外競價拍賣 5100 張 ， 競拍底價88 元，
最高投標張數 669 張，每股承銷價 105.6 元。評價上，參考同業有
6576 逸達以及 6838 台新藥，逸達生技近年股價區間 58-141 元，
股本 15.75 億元，市值區間 91.3-222 億元。台新藥近年股價區間
22-70 元，股本 15.1 億元，市值區間 33-106 億元。而泰合目前股
本 6 億元，參考同業市值區間，給予目標價 260 元，給予買進之投
資建議。

➢ 公司簡介： 
泰合生技成立於 2010 年 7 月，為新劑型新藥及特殊學名藥開發廠，藉由自
行開發之藥物傳輸平台技術結合現有藥物成份，將現有口服藥品成分改造為
透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型，以經皮吸收貼片與口溶膜藥品為代表 。公司專
注於開發 505(b)(2)NDA 新劑型新藥，並藉由 Transepithelial Delivery 
System (TDS)藥物傳輸技術平台改良現有藥物缺點，主要包括透皮及透黏膜
兩種不同藥物傳輸平台，劑型主要包含經皮吸收貼片、口溶膜與口頰溶膜三
類。產品線涵蓋抗血栓藥品、鴉片過量解毒及戒毒劑、小兒過動症、化療止
吐等治療領域。截至 2025 年，營收來自已授權之學名藥及受委託開發之專
案收入 99%。

抗凝血藥物市場: 
在全球醫療環境中，心血管疾病為主要致死與致殘原因之一，其發生率隨人
口老齡化及現代生活型態改變而持續上升。相關疾病涵蓋冠心病、高血壓及
高血脂等，並可能進一步導致心肌梗塞與中風等重大臨床事件。根據世界衛
生組織（WHO）統計，約有五分之四之心血管疾病死亡與心臟病發作及中
風相關，顯示其對全球公共健康之重大影響。

在臨床治療上，抗凝血藥物為心血管疾病管理中不可或缺之核心用藥之一，
主要用於預防及控制血栓形成，以降低心肌梗塞、中風及其他嚴重併發症之
風險。隨著民眾對心血管健康意識提升，以及疾病預防需求增加，抗凝血藥
物之臨床應用持續擴大，成為全球醫療體系中重要之基礎治療。 
在市場規模方面，全球抗凝血藥物市場於 2025 年約為 414.1 億美元，預計將
持續成長至 2034 年約 970.9 億美元 ， 年複合成長率約為9.93%， 顯示該市場

泰合產品介紹: 
➢ BEQIA(TAH3311 Apixaban ODF)抗血栓口溶膜: 
TAH3311(BEQIA)是全球首創的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥，以 BMS/Pfizer 開
發的 Eliquis 抗血栓錠劑為對照藥物。Eliquis 有效成分為 Apixaban，透過直
接抑制 Xa 凝血因子來治療和預防靜脈血栓 ，根據 BMS 財報 ，2025 年 Eliquis
全球銷售額達 144 億美元，其中美國及美國以外之銷售額分別達 102 億美元
和 42 億美元。Eliquis 佔 BMS 總營收約 29.8%，Eliquis 原料藥專利於 2028
年 10 月 17 日到期，劑型專利於 2031 年 8 月 24 日到期，為 505(b)(2)新劑
型新藥創造良好的切入時點，專利懸崖帶來營收的巨大挑戰，原廠已和解的幾
家學名藥廠預計最快將於 2028 年 4 月上市。Eliquis 除增加小兒用的粉劑外 ，
未開發出新劑型，而研發新一代的 FXIa 抗血栓藥數據臨床三期試驗療效及安
全性數據仍待觀察 ，並且抗凝血錠劑在具有吞嚥困難患者 使用痛點仍無法解
決。 
圖四：2Q26 Eliquis 營收及 2026 年財測

表一：2021 年後原廠 BMS/Pfizer 與 Apixaban 學名藥廠 P4 訴訟和解狀況

TAH3311(BEQIA)為泰合重點開發產品，其主要成分為 Apixaban，並採用口
溶膜（orodispersible film, ODF）劑型設計。Apixaban 透過選擇性抑制凝
血因子 Xa，可有效降低中風與全身性血栓風險，而降低非瓣膜性心房顫動
(NVAF)成年患者發生中風和全身性栓塞的風險是最大的應用，佔 Eliquis 總
銷售額的 70%。BEQIA 口溶膜鎖定改善高齡患者吞嚥困難的用藥痛點，相較
於 Eliquis 是一天吃兩錠需要搭配喝水，BEQIA 是一天吃兩片，僅需放入口
中即可快速溶解完成用藥，不須喝水、沒有噎嗆風險，優化長照與醫療機構
給藥流程。2025 年美國有 1,055 萬名心房顫動(AF)患者，其中約 75%之
791 萬名患者為 65 歲以上高齡患者，這當中 50~75%有吞嚥困難，故
BEQIA 在美國目標病患族群達 395.5 萬~593 萬人，隱含目標市場規模
110~164 億美元。

圖五：心房顫動(AF)：潛在的致命風險

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

圖八：BEQIA 授權模式: 原廠與非原廠雙軌並行

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

➢ TAH3341 長效劑型: 
在抗凝血治療市場中 ， 患者多為高齡且需長期甚至終生用藥族群 ， 臨床上普遍
存在多重用藥及吞嚥困難等問題，進而影響用藥便利性與治療依從性。儘管
Apixaban（Eliquis®）已為目前凝血因子 Xa 抑制劑中之領導產品， 
具備良好療效與安全性 ， 其每日兩次給藥之頻率及傳統錠劑劑型 ， 對部分患者
而言仍存在使用負擔 ， 顯示在既有標準治療基礎上 ， 透過劑型與給藥方式創新
以提升依從性，仍具明確之未被滿足醫療需求。 
TAH3341 為泰合延伸既有 Apixaban 產品線所開發之長效抗血栓口溶膜產
品，結合口溶膜（orodispersible film, ODF ）與先進緩釋技術，將原需每日
兩次服用之錠劑優化為 「每日一次」 之創新劑型。產品可於口腔中迅速崩解並
釋放藥物，使患者無需吞嚥或飲水即可完成給藥；同時，透過精密設計之控釋
機制 ， 使有效成分於體內持續穩定釋放 ， 維持適當血中濃度並達成長時間抗凝
效果。 此一 「快速崩解× 延長釋放」 之雙重設計，有效兼顧給藥便利性與療效
穩定性，並有助於降低血中濃度波動所帶來之潛在風險。 
在產品定位上，TAH3341 於維持 Apixaban 既有療效與安全性基礎上，進一
步透過降低給藥頻次與改善劑型使用方式 ， 顯著提升患者用藥依從性與長期治
療持續性。特別針對高齡族群、吞嚥困難患者及多重用藥族群，此一劑型可有
效降低給藥障礙並優化治療體驗。同時，產品亦有助於簡化用藥流程，降低照
護者負擔並提升整體用藥管理效率。TAH3341 透過 「標準治療藥物× 長效化
設計× 創新劑型平台」之整合策略，不僅回應現有抗凝血治療在依從性與便
利性方面之未被滿足需求 ， 亦進一步強化產品差異化與臨床價值 ， 有望於持續
成長之抗血栓市場中建立具競爭力之產品定位。

結論: 
泰合主力產品 BEQIA 專為吞嚥困難族群設計，以 Apixaban 為成分的抗血栓
口溶膜，鎖定高齡、吞嚥困難及需長期抗血栓治療族群。BEQIA 於 2026 年
6 月 22 日重新送件美國 FDA，進入 10 個月 PDUFA 審查期，2027 年 4 月
22 日為審核完成目標日期，可望於 2Q27 取得美國暫時性核准(Tentative 
Approval)。歐洲 EMA 方面，則於 2026 年 7 月 17 日完成 Day 121 關鍵清
單回覆，朝 CHMP 最終審查意見(Day 210)與 EC 核准推進，公司預計 4Q26
可望獲歐盟上市許可。授權策略方面，泰合採取原廠與非原廠雙軌並行。對
原廠而言，BEQIA 可借力其成熟通路快速推升口溶模市占，並在面臨專利懸
崖與學名藥競爭時，可藉此維持利潤表現，洽談時即以解決原廠現有困境的
定位切入。至於非原廠，泰合採精準區域分擊，鎖定主要市場以外的區域優

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

## Document 321: FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus FEMO's Steady Climb

Source PDF: all report pdf/FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus  FEMO's Steady Climb.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus  FEMO's Steady Climb
Date:    5 hours ago
FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus FEMO's Steady Climb

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus
FEMO's Steady Climb
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 19 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Jonny Gios / Unsplash
I. FOMO vs FEMO
Forecasting the stock market is easy! Just multiply the P/E (price-to-
earnings ratio) by E (earnings per share) to get P (the stock price index).
The hard part, of course, is getting the P/E and E right. We call the P/E
component FOMO (fear of missing out) and the E component FEMO
(fabulous earnings momentum). This year, investors have been on a
rollercoaster ride as FOMO has fluctuated mostly to the downside, while

FEMO has continued climbing.

### Page 3

More recently, concerns about AI investment returns and rising US
Treasury yields have tightened credit conditions, raising fears about the
sustainability of the AI investment boom and weighing on P/Es. Since
early October, multiple compression has outpaced continued earnings
gains, dragging many stocks lower.
Semiconductors have been at the center of the recent rout, with the
semiconductor stock price index plunging 15.7% over eight consecutive
sessions. However, FOMO staged a comeback today, with the Nasdaq

rising 1.1% and semiconductors gaining 1.6%, snapping their losing
streak. The FOMO rollercoaster continues, but FEMO remains firmly on
an upward trajectory.
Here is more:
(1) S&P 500. So far this year, forward earnings surged 31.0% ytd through
October 19, more than offsetting a 14.2% contraction in the forward P/E.
Consequently, the index is up 12.3% ytd (chart).
(2) Information Technology. The divergence is even more striking in the
S&P 500 Information Technology sector. Forward earnings has soared

65.5% ytd, while the forward P/E has contracted 25.6%, leaving the
sector's stock price index up 23.0% ytd (chart). Since early October,
however, falling P/Es have outpaced rising earnings, pulling IT stock
prices lower.

### Page 4

(3) Semiconductors. FEMO is incredibly strong in the S&P 500
Semiconductors industry, which now accounts for a record 55.0% of the IT
sector's forward earnings and 40.9% of its market capitalization (chart).
That implies a significant forward P/E discount to the rest of the IT sector
and to the S&P 500. The discount reflects concerns that rapidly rising
borrowing costs for highly leveraged AI infrastructure providers could slow
the AI investment boom and weaken demand for chips. Semiconductor

earnings continue to soar, but that's because capacity constraints are
boosting semiconductor prices, which should fall sooner or later as either
demand slows or capacity expands, or both.
(4) Healthy constraints. We view investors' growing concerns about the
AI boom as good news. Financial, social, political, and physical
constraints should reduce overinvestment risks and keep the boom on a
sustainable path. Recently, financial constraints have been in focus, with

### Page 5

CoreWeave's bond yields surging above 15% last week as the Bond
Vigilantes increasingly scrutinized AI borrowers. The bond market is
playing a welcome role in allocating capital more efficiently. Meanwhile,
the big hyperscalers remain the boom's main drivers, supported by strong
cash flows and ample access to capital. The AI rollercoaster may be
hitting speed bumps, but that will help keep it on a steadier path.
II. Industrial Production
The strength of the US economy continues to underpin strong earnings

growth. Industrial production rose 0.7% m/m in September, its biggest
gain since April, and 2.2% y/y, the strongest since September 2022
(charts). Leading the way higher was utilities output. That reflects rising
electricity demand from data centers and manufacturing, as well as
weather-related demand. Manufacturing output edged up 0.3%.

### Page 6

Since ChatGPT was released in late 2022, technology-related industrial
production has risen sharply to record highs (chart).
Defense and space equipment production also continues to increase at a
solid pace, supported by rising military spending and the rapid growth of
the space industry (chart).

### Page 7

III. Federal Deficit
Fiscal policy also continues to boost economic growth, though at the cost
of large federal deficits and mounting federal debt. The federal budget
deficit totaled $1.99 trillion over the 12 months through September, while
marketable Treasury debt increased by $2.12 trillion (chart).
Federal outlays reached $7.40 trillion, including $3.77 trillion for health
care, Medicare, and Social Security (chart). Notably, net interest payments

reached a record $1.05 trillion, exceeding defense spending of $970
billion.

### Page 8

Tax receipts totaled $5.40 trillion, supported by rising individual income
and payroll taxes as well as tariff revenues (chart). Corporate income tax
receipts have been depressed this year by the 100% depreciation
allowance granted by last year's One Big Beautiful Bill Act (OBBB).
IV. Foreign Capital Flows
Given Corporate America's FEMO, it is not surprising that foreign capital
continues to pour into US financial markets. Over the 12 months through

August, net foreign purchases of US equities totaled a whopping $1.01
trillion, followed by corporate bonds and Treasury notes and bonds (chart).
Apparently, foreign investors have a greater appetite for US equities and
corporate bonds than for US Treasuries.

### Page 9

Net foreign equity inflows surged to a record high, far exceeding previous
peaks (chart). Private investors accounted for $844 billion, while foreign
official institutions contributed another $165 billion, a remarkable
development given their historically limited participation in US equity
markets.
Foreigners purchased more US corporate bonds than US Treasury notes
and bonds over the past 12 months (chart).

### Page 10

clause.

## Document 349: Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend (2026-10-21)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend (2026-10-21).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Key points
• Index growth rate holds at –0.03% in

• Momentum is a touch below trend
heading into late 2026/early 2027.
• Mixed detail suggests the economy
lacking a clear direction.

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, held at –0.03% in September,
unchanged from August.

The Leading Index continues to show moderately improved
momentum compared to the middle of the year, with the
latest reads pointing to growth holding at or slightly below
trend heading into late 2026/early 2027. Growth appears
to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling. That
said, both the latest fuel prices and the RBA’s September
interest rate increase are still working their way through to
the economy with impacts likely to take some of the gloss off

growth near term. Overall, Westpac expects annual growth
to come in at 1.5% for calendar 2026, a below-trend result.
The Leading Index growth rate has firmed slightly over the
last six months, rising from –0.16% back in March to –0.03%
in September. The component detail shows mixed moves over
the period. Four components have contributed more positively
with reduced drags from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Unemployment Expectations Index (adding +0.23ppts to

the Index growth rate); the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Expectations Index (adding another +0.15ppts);
and the S&P/ASX200 (+0.15ppts) combining with a modest
lift in growth in US industrial production (adding +0.12ppts).
These positives have been partially offset by modest drags
from four previously stable components: dwelling approvals
(–0.34ppts); monthly hours worked (–0.08ppts); commodity
prices, measured in AUD terms (–0.07ppts) and the yield

spread (–0.05ppts).
Overall, the component mix speaks to a lack of direction.
Indeed, despite some large shocks over the last year, the
Leading Index growth rate has been relatively contained since
early 2025, holding in a narrow –0.3% to +0.5% range. This
compares to history which, outside of the COVID and GFC
shocks, has seen growth rates move between a much wider
–1.9% to +1.6% range. Of course, the apparent stability,
which is evident across all components of the index, may not

last but for now it suggests the economy has been stuck in a
bit of a holding pattern over the last two years.
Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines
variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken

individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting
**Please note that this will be the last Westpac press release for
the monthly Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index. We will
continue to publish regular bulletin updates through our westpaciq.
com.au website.

## Document 382: SpaceX Q3 AI业务大爆发、收入暴增830%，利润碾压预期，资本支出近200亿｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/SpaceX Q3 AI业务大爆发、收入暴增830%，利润碾压预期，资本支出近200亿｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

三季度， SpaceX 总营收同⽐增 161% ⾄近 146 亿美元，净利润 44 亿，分别较分析师预期⾼
14% 和 134% ，资本⽀出较营收⾼逾三成、基本符合预期；云服务、收购 Cursor 和 Grok 应
⽤增⻓推动 AI 业务超级智能（ SI ）收⼊增⾄ 75.6 亿美元，⾸次成为公司最⼤收⼊源，较预
期⾼ 20% ，业务资本⽀出 164 亿、占总⽀出 84% ；互联业务收⼊同⽐增 69% ，星链⽤⼾增
⾄ 1360 万；航天业务收⼊增 6% ，星舰⾸次贡献收⼊，但研发投⼊继续拖累，业务运营亏损
700 万美元。财报后，股价盘后转涨，⼀度涨近 7% 。
摘要：
上市以来第⼆份季度财报显⽰，⻢斯克旗下 SpaceX 今年第三季度取得了碾压华尔街预期的亮眼
成绩。
美东时间 20 ⽇周⼆美股盘后， SpaceX 公布，截⾄ 9 ⽉的 2026 年第三季度，公司营收同⽐增⻓
161% ⾄ 145.8 亿美元，较分析师预期的约 128 亿美元⾼ 10% 以上；净利润约 44 亿美元，为分析师

预期 18.8 亿美元的 2.3 倍；调整后 EBITDA 利润同⽐⼤增 306% ⾄ 88.1 亿美元，较预期⾼逾 30% 。
这份财报最突出的变化是， SpaceX 的增⻓引擎已经从传统⽕箭发射和 “ 星链 ” （ Starlink ）互联业
务，进⼀步转向 AI 领域业务 “ 超级智能 ” （下称 SI ）。三季度 SI 收⼊达到 75.6 亿美元，同⽐暴增
830% ，较分析师预期的 63 亿美元⾼ 20% ，并⾸次成为公司最⼤收⼊来源。
相⽐⼆季度， SpaceX 增⻓斜率明显抬升。⼆季度公司营收同⽐增⻓ 92% ，净亏损 5.41 亿美元，
虽较⼀年前 10 亿美元的亏损明显收窄，但仍未实现盈利；当季调整后 EBITDA 为 35 亿美元，同⽐
增⻓ 191% 。到了三季度，公司不仅营收同⽐增速跃升⾄ 161% ，还实现 40 亿多美元净利润，调
整后 EBITDA 环⽐也增⻓约 149% 。
财报公布前，美东时间 10 ⽉ 20 ⽇周⼆， SpaceX （ SPCX ）股价收跌约 0.1% 。盘后 SpaceX 公布

财报后，股价迅速转涨，盘后⼀度涨超 2% 。市场反应的核⼼在于： SI 收⼊和利润释放速度超预
期，抵消了资本开⽀和成本继续⾼企带来的压⼒。
业绩电话会期间，随着⻢斯克等公司⾼管透露明年年度经常性收⼊（ ARR ）最⾼ 1 万亿美元、实
现 13GW AI 算⼒等⽬标或指引，还确认在讨论与特斯拉合并， SpaceX 股价进⼀步⾛⾼，盘后涨
幅曾接近 7% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

核⼼指标：营收、利润、 EBITDA 全⾯超预期，但成本也⾼于预期
从彭博提供的分析师预期看， SpaceX 三季度⼏乎在收⼊和利润端全⾯跑赢市场预期。
指标 三季度实际 分析师预期 对⽐
营收 145.8 亿美元 约 128 亿美元 超预期约 14%
SI/AI 收⼊ 75.6 亿美元 63 亿美元 超预期约 20%
运营利润 27.9 亿美元 22.4 亿美元 超预期约 25%
每股收益（ EPS ） 0.35 美元 0.15 美元 超预期逾⼀倍
净利润 44.0 亿美元 18.8 亿美元 超预期约 134%
调整后 EBITDA 88.1 亿美元 65.9 亿美元 超预期约 34%
资本开⽀ 195.9 亿美元 196 亿美元 基本符合预期
SI 资本开⽀ 164.4 亿美元 169 亿美元 略低于预期
总成本与开⽀ 117.8 亿美元 112.3 亿美元 ⾼于预期约 5%
公司三季度总营收从去年同期的 55.8 亿美元增⾄ 145.8 亿美元；总成本与开⽀则从 58.5 亿美元增

⾄ 117.8 亿美元。虽然成本⾼于预期，但收⼊扩张更快，带动运营利润从去年同期亏损 2.73 亿美
元转为盈利 27.9 亿美元，运营利润率达到约 19% 。
调整后 EBITDA 为 88.1 亿美元，对应调整后 EBITDA 利润率约 60% 。需要注意的是，调整后
EBITDA 为⾮ GAAP 指标，剔除了折旧摊销、股权激励、并购相关费⽤等项⽬，因此其改善幅度
明显⾼于 GAAP ⼝径下运营利润。
SI 成最⼤收⼊来源：云服务、 Cursor 和 Grok 推动 AI 业务爆发
SpaceX 财报中最关键的变量是 SI 业务。公司注释称， SI 即此前披露的⼈⼯智能（ Artificial
Intelligence ）业务。
三季度 SI 收⼊ 75.6 亿美元，环⽐增⻓ 195% ，同⽐增⻓ 830% ，已经超过互联（ Connectivity ）业
务的 52.5 亿美元，成为 SpaceX 第⼀⼤业务板块。其中：

### Page 3

⼴告收⼊ 5.38 亿美元，同⽐增⻓约 19% ；
SI 解决⽅案与基础设施收⼊ 70.22 亿美元，去年同期仅 3.59 亿美元；
公司称， SI 解决⽅案和基础设施收⼊同⽐增加 67 亿美元，主要来⾃新的云服务协议、 Cursor
收购，以及 Grok 模型和软件应⽤增⻓。
盈利能⼒也出现拐点。 SI 三季度运营利润 4.31 亿美元，⽽⼆季度仍亏损 12.57 亿美元，去年同期
亏损 17.22 亿美元。公司披露，三季度 SI 运营利润中包含 Cursor 收购带来的 12 亿美元股权激励费
⽤。若按公司⼝径， SI 调整后 EBITDA 达到 49.53 亿美元，去年同期为亏损 4.28 亿美元。
算⼒扩张仍是 SI 业务的核⼼。 SpaceX 三季度末名义算⼒达到 1.7GW ，⾼于⼆季度末的 1.4GW ，
也远⾼于去年同期的 0.6GW 。公司称，增⻓来⾃ COLOSSUS II 继续建设，并在季度内签署更多
云服务协议，增加⾯向第三⽅客⼾的 SI 基础设施容量储备。
Cursor 也是本季度财报的重点。 SpaceX 于 8 ⽉ 14 ⽇完成对 Cursor 的收购，隐含股权价值 600 亿

美元， GAAP 购买对价 425 亿美元，并发⾏与未来服务相关的 234 亿美元股权奖励。⾃并表⽇⾄ 9
⽉ 30 ⽇， Cursor 为 SpaceX 贡献 6.08 亿美元收⼊。
互联：星链⽤⼾增⾄ 1360 万，企业和政府收⼊增⻓更快
互联（ Connectivity ）仍是 SpaceX 最稳定的利润来源。三季度互联收⼊ 52.48 亿美元，环⽐增⻓
22% ，同⽐增⻓ 69% ；运营利润 23.67 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 99% ；调整后 EBITDA 为 34.99 亿美元，
同⽐增⻓ 82% 。
分项看，星链消费端仍在扩张：

### Page 4

消费者收⼊ 31.21 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 57% ；
企业与政府收⼊ 21.27 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 91% ；
星链消费订阅⽤⼾达到 1360 万，环⽐增加 160 万，去年同期为 720 万；
消费端⽉度 ARPU 为 69 美元，较⼆季度的 66 美元环⽐回升约 5% ，但低于去年同期的 83 美元。
这意味着，星链⽤⼾规模继续快速增⻓，但单⽤⼾收⼊仍低于去年⽔平。公司正在通过企业、
政府、航空、海事等⾼价值场景弥补消费端 ARPU 下滑。截⾄三季度末，已有超过 3800 架⻜机
搭载星链服务。
互联业务的成本也在上升。该板块总成本和开⽀同⽐增加 9.74 亿美元，主要来⾃卫星星座扩张、
星链终端交付以及⽀持收⼊增⻓的营销投⼊。但由于收⼊增⻓更快，该板块运营利润率达到约
45% ，仍是 SpaceX 当前最成熟的现⾦利润来源。
航天：收⼊增⻓相⽐其他业务温和，星舰⾸次贡献收⼊但研发投⼊继续拖
累利润
传统的航天（ Space ）业务三季度收⼊ 17.67 亿美元，环⽐增⻓ 84% ，同⽐增⻓ 6% ，⼤致与分析

师预期的约 18 亿美元业务收⼊⼀致。
不过，从运营数据看，发射数量并没有增⻓。三季度 SpaceX 完成：
客⼾发射 12 次，去年同期 15 次；
内部发射 23 次，去年同期 29 次；
总发射 35 次，去年同期 44 次；
⼊轨质量 476 吨，去年同期 525 吨。
也就是说，航天业务收⼊增⻓并⾮来⾃发射次数增加，⽽更多来⾃发射服务结构、开发收⼊以
及 “ 星舰 ” （ Starship ）相关进展。
三季度最重要的业务⾥程碑是星舰⾸次收⼊产⽣任务。当季， SpaceX 完成星舰第 14 次⻜⾏，也
是⾸次轨道级星舰⻜⾏，将 26 颗星链 V3 卫星送⼊宽带星座。公司称，该任务增加的下⾏容量约
为⼀次 Falcon 9 星链发射的 10 倍。

### Page 5

但星舰研发仍在侵蚀航天业务利润。三季度航天业务运营亏损 700 万美元，⼆季度亏损 5.42 亿美
元，去年同期则盈利 2.59 亿美元。公司称，航天业务总成本和开⽀同⽐增加 3.67 亿美元，主要因
为继续加速星舰项⽬研发投⼊。
利润质量：净利润⾼于运营利润，⾮经营项⽬贡献不⼩
SpaceX 三季度运营利润为 27.91 亿美元，但净利润达到 44.04 亿美元，⾼于运营利润本⾝。这说
明净利润不仅来⾃主业改善，也受到⾮经营项⽬和税项的明显⽀撑。
三季度公司录得：
利息收⼊ 8.73 亿美元；
利息费⽤ 6.75 亿美元；
其他收⼊净额 10.25 亿美元；
所得税收益 3.90 亿美元。
这些项⽬合计对净利润形成正向贡献。因此，虽然 GAAP ⼝径下净利润⼤幅超预期，但投资者仍
需区分 “ 主业经营改善 ” 和 “ 财务及税项因素 ” 对净利润的贡献。
此外，股权激励费⽤仍然较⾼。三季度公司普通股权激励费⽤ 11.46 亿美元，并购相关股权激励
费⽤ 12.17 亿美元，合计超过 23 亿美元。这些费⽤在调整后 EBITDA 中被剔除，但会影响 GAAP

利润和股东摊薄。
三季度稀释后加权平均股本为 124.75 亿股，去年同期为 29.41 亿股，反映 IPO 、股权融资、并购
对价及员⼯激励带来的显著摊薄。在此背景下，公司稀释后 EPS 仍达到 0.35 美元，⾼于预期的
0.15 美元。
资本⽀出较营收⾼逾三成， SI 算⼒建设仍是现⾦消耗核⼼
SpaceX 三季度资本开⽀ 195.93 亿美元，⼏乎与分析师预期的 196 亿美元⼀致，但绝对值极⾼，
较当季营收⾼约 34% 。其中 SI 业务资本开⽀占总资本开⽀约 84% ，略低于分析师预期的 169 亿美
元。
分业务板块看：
航天业务资本开⽀ 16.06 亿美元；
互联业务资本开⽀ 15.43 亿美元；
SI 资本开⽀ 164.44 亿美元。
这显⽰ SpaceX 当前资本开⽀⼏乎完全围绕 AI/SI 算⼒基础设施展开。 SI 单季收⼊ 75.6 亿美元，但
资本开⽀达到 164.4 亿美元，说明公司仍处在明显的提前建设产能阶段。
从前三季度看， SpaceX 经营活动现⾦流为 130.97 亿美元，但同期资本开⽀达到 480.69 亿美元，

简单计算⾃由现⾦流约为负 349.7 亿美元。公司增⻓仍需要资本市场和债务融资⽀持。
资产负债表⽅⾯， SpaceX 三季度末现⾦及现⾦等价物 512.94 亿美元，另有可交易证券 385.61 亿
美元，合计接近 899 亿美元。债务和融资租赁合计约 388 亿美元，公司仍处于净现⾦状态。前三

• The global landscape remains complex, shaped by AI, 
energy prices and tariffs. 
• Taiwan’s AI cycle remains strong, with robust AI 
exports and investment and an intact semiconductor 
competitive moat. 
• AI is generating broader domestic spillovers, with 
wealth effects and wage growth supporting 
consumption. 
• Taiwan is cushioning the energy shock by diversifying 
energy imports and absorbing much of the increase in 
upstream costs. 
• AI-driven growth is likely to moderate from 4Q26 into 
2027, as hyperscaler  cash-flow pressures rise and 
higher global rates lift investment costs. 
• Energy remains a n inflation risk, with domestic 
electricity prices facing upward pressure after the 
November local elections. 
• GDP growth is expected to moderate to high -single 
digits from 4Q26, while we maintain our 11.6%/5.6% 
GDP forecasts for 2026/27. 
• Inflation is likely to remain above 2% in 4Q26, with our 
2.0%/2.1% CPI forecasts for 2026/27 unchanged. 
• We maintain our call for CBC to hike 12.5bp to 2.125% 
in December, amid tighter liquidity conditions. 
• Housing should recover gradually despite credit 
easing, as excess new -home inventory remains a n 
overhang.

Taiwan: AI, energy and 4Q outlook October 13, 2026

Global backdrop

The global landscape remains complex, 
shaped by the interplay of AI, energy 
prices and tariffs, with significant 
implications for growth, inflation and 
financial conditions.

1) AI

The debate over the durability of the AI 
investment boom continues.  Tailwinds 
include technological advances, broader 
adoption, and s trategic and geopolitical 
considerations. Headwinds range from 
uncertainty over investment returns  and 
hyperscaler financing pressure to 
physical bottlenecks , safety concerns, 
and regulatory uncertainty.

Hyperscaler financing pressure has 
attracted increasing attention . In the 
early stages of the AI investment boom, 
hyperscaler capex was largely financed 
by internal cash flow. This is now 
changing, as free cash flow is expected 
to turn negative from 4Q, requiring major 
players to rely on debt issuance and 
other external financing increasingly.

At the same time, higher global interest 
rates are raising the cost of AI 
investment. The Fed resumed rate hikes 
at its September meeting, while global 
bond yields remain under upward 
pressure amid persistent inflation and 
elevated public and private borro wing 
needs. Our baseline assumes US rates 
will rise by 50bps this year and a further 
25bps in 2027 to 4.50%, with global 
long-term rates also remaining under 
pressure. This could increasingly weigh 
on the economics and pace of AI 
investment.

US-China AI competition is also drawing 
increasing attention.  Across the five 
layers of the AI ecosystem, China is 
making rapid progress in energy, 
infrastructure, AI models and 
applications, supported by its large scale, 
cost-effective domestic manufacturing 
supply chains and huge consumer 
market, which provides a wide range of 
real-world scenarios for AI adoption. The 
emergence of a more integrated and 
cost-competitive Chinese AI ecosystem 
could increasingly challe nge the US 
ecosystem, which currently operates at a 
relatively higher cost.

China, however, remains relatively weak 
in semiconductors.  Around half of 
China's domestic AI chip market still 
relies on foreign suppliers, including 
lower-end NVIDIA chips. This 
semiconductor bottleneck remains a key 
constraint on China's AI development. In 
our view, Taiwanese semiconductor 
companies' competiti ve moat therefore 
remains largely intact in the near term.

or semiconductor demand.  Hyperscaler

through 2Q, while Bloomberg forecasts 
growth to moderate from 95% this year 
to 39% in 2027. Similarly, global 
semiconductor sales remained strong 
through 3Q, while WSTS expects growth 
to moderate from 90% this year to 27 % 
in 2027.

2) Energy

The US -Iran conflict remains at a 
standoff, keeping the energy -price 
outlook highly uncertain.  New 
developments are likely after the US 
midterm elections in November, with 
both further escalation and de -
escalation possible. Crude oil prices have 
remained elevated at around 
USD100/bbl amid a persistent 
geopolitical risk premium . In contrast, 
refined product prices have risen more 
sharply as refining disruptions and 
tighter product logistics have amplified 
the supply shock. Natural gas prices 
have also increased sharply, reflecting 
disruptions to LNG supply and limited 
alternative shipping routes.

Taiwan has been actively diversifying 
its energy import sources to cushion the 
impact. LNG imports from Qatar have 
fallen to zero since April, while imports 
from the US and other suppliers have 
surged to offset the shortfall, helping 
maintain stable domestic electricity 
supply.

3) Tariffs

US tariff actions are likely to accelerate 
after the US midterm elections in 
November. Two major tariff measures 
remain in the pipeline. The Section 301 
forced-labor tariff was implemented in 
July, while final tariff action under the 
Section 301 overcapacity investigation, 
which covers 16 economies, remains 
pending. Meanwhile, Phase 1 of the 
Section 232 semiconductor tariffs was 
implemented in January, while Phase 2, 
covering a broader range of 
semiconductor-related products, 
remains pending.

Taiwan has secured a relatively 
favourable framework through its trade 
and investment agreements with the 
US. Taiwan signed an Agreement on 
Reciprocal Trade (ART) and an 
Investment MOU with the US earlier this 
year. The ART establishes a 15% US tariff 
rate on Taiwanese goods, while the MOU 
provides Taiwan with preferential 
treatment under Section 232, including 
for semiconductors. The ART has yet to 
enter into force, and it remains to be 
seen how the final Section 301 tariff 
burden will interact with the 15% ART 
framework. Meanwhile, major Taiwanese 
companies such as TSMC are expanding 
their US investments under the 
Investment MOU, which could support 
relatively favourable treatment for 
Taiwan when broader Section 232 
semiconductor tariffs are introduced.

Taiwan 4Q outlook

Taiwan has navigated the complex 
global environment relatively well so far 
this year, supported by robust AI exports 
and investment, diversified energy 
imports, and efforts to absorb higher 
upstream energy costs. Some 
normalisation is likely in 4Q26 –2027, as 
the AI -driven growth boost moderates 
and domestic energy prices face greater 
pressure to adjust.

1) Growth

GDP growth remains broadly on track 
with our expectations. High -frequency 
indicators suggest that GDP growth 
should remain around 12% yoy in 3Q, 
broadly in line with 12.9% in 2Q but below 
the 15.4% peak in 1Q. Growth is expected 
to moderate to a high -single-digit pace 
from 4Q onwards. We maintain our full -
year GDP growth forecasts at 11.6% for 
2026 and 5.6% for 2027.

The AI cycle remains the key driver of 
Taiwan's growth.  Information and 
communication products and electronic 
components accounted for nearly 80% 
of total exports in the first nine months 
of this year, while manufacturing 
accounts for nearly 50% of gross fixed

capital formation. From 4Q onwards, 
export growth is expected to moderate 
from exceptionally strong levels to a 
more normal but still robust pace, 
broadly following the trajectory of 
global hyperscaler capex and 
semiconductor sales. Investment is likely 
to follow a similar path.

For now, Taiwanese technology 
companies continue to report robust 
revenue growth, led by semiconductors, 
ODMs/EMS, and PCs and servers. 
Capacity utilisation at leading Taiwanese 
foundries has also continued to rise, 
suggesting that investment demand 
remains strong.

The AI boom is also beginning to 
generate broader spillovers to the 
domestic economy. The AI-driven equity 
rally has generated significant wealth 
effects, with the TAIEX up 70% year-to-
date and Taiwan becoming the world's 
fifth-largest stock market. Taiwanese 
households have benefited from the 
appreciation of financial assets, with 
equities and mutual funds accounting for 
nearly 30% of total household financial 
assets. The equity rally has also 
generated positive income effects. Wage 
growth has picked up, led by the services 
sector, particularly financial services, as 
strong earnings at financial 
institutions—especially securities 
firms—have prompted higher 
compensation to retain talent. These 
wealth and income gains should support 
household consumption, creating a 
positive feedback loop that broadens the 
benefits of the AI boom across the wider 
economy.

                                                                                                                                            7

2) Inflation

CPI inflation is also tracking broadly in 
line with our forecast. Headline CPI 
remained elevated at 2.4% yoy in 3Q, up 
from 1.2% in 1Q and 2.2% in 2Q. We 
maintain our forecast for headline CPI 
inflation to remain close to 2.5% in 4Q, 
and retain our full-year forecasts at 2.0% 
for 2026 and 2.1% for 2027.

Energy inflation remains the main 
upside risk.  The government has 
absorbed much of the increase in energy 
costs through state -owned CPC and 
Taipower, limiting the pass -through to 
domestic consumers. With losses at both 
companies widening, the government 
will need to decide whether to provide 
additional subsidies to cover the losses 
or allow further increases in domestic 
energy prices. The September re view 
postponed the adjustment to the 
December meeting, when the overall 
situation will be reassessed after the 
November local elections. Taipower's  
latest review indicated that electricity 
rates would need to rise by 12.8%. A hike 
in electricity prices could lift inflation 
expectations and generate second -
round effects, particularly as the labour 
market tightens and wage pressures 
increase.

3) Monetary policy

The central bank kept the policy 
discount rate unchanged at 2.00% at its 
September meeting, in line with our 
expectations. We continue to expect a 
modest 12.5bp rate hike at the 
December meeting, bringing the policy 
rate to 2.125%.  By then, CPI inflation is 
likely to have remained above 2% for 
around six consecutive months. The 
possibility of an increase in electricity 
prices following the November local 
elections could further lift inflation 
expectations, strengthening the case for 
a December rate hike.

Financial conditions have already 
tightened despite the absence of a 
policy rate hike.  M2 growth remained 
around 7% yoy in 3Q, well below double-
digit nominal GDP growth. At the same 
time, bank loan growth has accelerated 
to more than 10% yoy, driven by strong

Taiwan: AI, energy and 4Q outlook October 13, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            8

corporate credit demand, particularly AI-
related fundraising by technology 
companies. Tighter financial conditions 
are also evident in rising market interest 
rates, including commercial paper rates 
and government bond yields.

The central bank has continued to ease 
housing credit controls gradually. At its 
September meeting, it raised the LTV 
cap on second -home mortgage loans to 
70%, the second easing of housing credit 
controls this year. There is scope for 
further easing, including removing the 
LTV cap on second-home loans or raising 
the LTV caps for third - and fourth-home 
loans and high -value properties, which 
currently stand at 30%. However, the 
central bank is likely to proceed 
cautiously, given concerns that a faster 
relaxation could reignite  speculative 
demand and put renewed upward 
pressure on housing prices.

4) Housing

A gradual and modest recovery in the 
housing market is expected from 4Q26 
into 2027.  The existing -home market 
has already shown early signs of 
recovery, with Sinyi  housing prices 
returning to slightly positive yoy growth 
since 2Q. Total building transactions also 
returned to positive yoy growth in 3Q. 
The easing of credit controls should help 
reinforce this modest recovery trend.

The new -home market remains under 
pressure. The number of new units under 
construction has started to decline since 
2023, while new completions are  only 
beginning to ease this year . Demand 
remains weak, as potential buyers 
continue to wait for lower prices. Unsold 
new homes stood at 113K units at end -
2025, compared with a historical normal 
level of around 80K, implying excess 
inventory of roughly 30K units. At the 
current pace of new -home transactions, 
it would take roughly one year to 
normalize the existing excess inventory, 
and longer when factoring in the new 
supply pipeline. Some industry experts 
estimate that it could take as long as 3.8 
years digest current inventories fully 
(here).

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Retail sales growth is expected to 
accelerate from 4.5% yoy in July to 7.8% 
in August, supported by an increase in 
visitor arrivals. The daily average of 
mainland visitor arrivals rebounded from 
2.7% yoy in July to 13.3% in August. This 
significantly outpaced the growth in 
outbound tourism  to 10.7% in August. 
The overall diffusion index improved 
during the month, with both the retail 
trade and restaurant sectors showing 
gains. Consumer prices remained steady 
at 1.7% yoy in August, while the prices of 
durable goods and food improved to 0.8% 
and 0.6% yoy, respectively.

Indonesia

September inflation is expected to stay 
high but not accelerate sharply from 
August. Headline CPI inflation is 
projected to rise marginally to 3.3% yoy 
in September from 3.2% in August. 
Continued rise in food prices, notably 
rice and perishables, is expected to be 
offset by steady subsidised fuel costs. 
Official price stabilisation measures and 
inter-province supply adjustments will 
help to limit the spillover impact of El 
Niño risks. Evolving inflation dynamics 
are consisted with the BI’s decision to 
pause on rates, shifting attention instead 
towards managing near -term rupiah 
volatility and external risks.

South Korea

September trade and inflation data will 
be the key focus in the week ahead. 
Exports are expected to remain strong at 
around 60% yoy, supported by front -
loading of shipments ahead of the 
Chuseok holiday (78.3% growth in the 
first 20 days of September). Headline 
CPI is expected to remain around 3% yoy, 
driven by festive demand for food and 
services ahead of Chuseok. We maintain 
our forecast for the Bank of Korea to 
deliver one more 25bps rate hike to 3.25% 
in 4Q. We do not rule out an additional 
25bps hike to 3.50% if elevated global 
oil prices prove m ore persistent, adding 
further upside pressure to CPI in 4Q.

Singapore

We expect Singapore’s industrial 
production growth to surge to 19.0% yoy 
in August 2026, sharply higher than the 
6.8% yoy  in July. This was partly 
flattered by favourable base effects, as 
August 2025 marked the lowest monthly 
reading of that year. The city -state’s 
factory expansion continued to be driven 
primarily by strength in electronics and 
precision engineering, both of  which are 
benefitting from the global artificial 
intelligence (AI) infrastructure build -out 
and sustained demand for AI hardware.

Vietnam 
We expect Vietnam’s real GDP growth to 
accelerate to 9.4% yoy in 3Q26, stepping 
up from 8.4% yoy  in 2Q26. This growth 
surge was driven by broad -based 
strength across electronics production 
and exports, foreign and public

### Page 3

The Week Ahead September 25, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

investments, and retail spending, 
notwithstanding evolving geopolitical 
developments. The stronger momentum 
was corroborated by high -frequency 
indicators from July -August, while 
September’s data will likely be robust. In 
July-August, i ndustrial production 
expanded by an average of 14.9% yoy 
(from 11.2% yoy average in 2Q26), goods 
exports grew by 25.5% yoy (gaining from

remained robust, alongside  resilient 
domestic and tourism spending, even as 
inflation remained elevated. 
Consequently, we see upside risks to our 
already-bullish 2026 GDP growth 
forecast of 8.0%.

## Document 436: ‘Cost-Plus’ Costs (2026-10-20)

Source PDF: all report pdf/‘Cost-Plus’ Costs (2026-10-20).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #labour #rba #productivity
‘Cost-Plus’ Costs
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
10:40 October 16 2026 Share
Entrenched expectations that cost increases can and should be passed on are precisely
what tight monetary policy is leaning against, but only indirectly.
The recent tendency of some firms to bundle into prices card-acceptance costs that had previously
been surcharged – and then some, in some cases – demonstrates a ‘cost-plus’ approach to pricing.

### Page 2

The recent banning of card surcharges has caused considerable upset this month, even though it is a
reversion to historical and international practice. The card schemes used to impose “no-surcharge”
rules, until the RBA banned these in 2003, and many other countries still have them. The turnaround
represents the RBA throwing in the towel on its longstanding effort to use price incentives to get people
to choose low-cost payment methods. Surcharges no longer made sense in a largely cashless
economy. In any case, least-cost routing puts the power to choose lower-cost methods in the hands of
the merchants who actually bear the cost.

In most cases, firms will simply roll the surcharge into the sticker price, leaving the total cost to the
consumer unchanged, just more transparent. We also understand that some large retailers have
absorbed the cost – if they were surcharging in the first place, which many did not. (The RBA estimated
that around 16% of businesses imposed surcharges.) Inevitably, though, some firms have lifted their
prices by more than any reasonable estimate of the merchant service fee that the surcharge was
meant to cover. And because this is Australia, of course takeaway coffee has been the centre of
consumer discontent.

Higher prices for one’s caffeine fix was unlikely to be the main instigator of this month’s slump in
consumer sentiment. Petrol prices are typically more salient for cost-of-living concerns, and the before-
and-after picture on the RBA announcement that week shows that higher interest rates also contributed
to the decline.

In some senses, though, the post-surcharge prices and the RBA’s decisions are linked: businesses
passing on increased costs into their own prices – and expecting to be able to make that increase stick
– is exactly the kind of inflationary environment the RBA is leaning against. Firms have been subjected
to an array of non-labour cost escalations, from pandemic-era supply chain issues to electricity to fuel
to water to building supplies. And IT equipment prices, which for decades were a reliable source of cost
reductions, are nowadays flat to up. Passing these costs on, leaving margins intact, demonstrates that
demand is not weak enough to prevent this, and signals that firms’ inflationary expectations might be
higher than the RBA desires. The RBA will take this as a sign that demand is still outstripping supply.

To the extent they can pass on these costs through to prices, it suggests that demand was strong
enough to bear it. The RBA will take this as a signal that demand is still outstripping supply.
This is mostly a story about non-labour costs. Base wages growth has been moderate for some
years, though the above-average increase in award wages this year will add a little. Not all firms will
seek to pass these cost increases on, but some will, including because they have limited scope to
absorb the cost increase into margins.
A cost-plus mentality has drawbacks, including that it limits the imagination away from finding
alternative, efficiency-improving responses. It also accords with recent academic research
suggesting that firms form expectations and set prices according to what is happening in their own
industry and experience, while putting little weight on economy-wide inflation or central bank
credibility. This is an unpleasant message for many consumers or anyone in an interest-sensitive
sector when supply shocks abound.

### Page 3

This is mostly a story about non-labour costs. While the Fair Work Commission did hand down an
above-average increase in award wages this year and this was probably passed through to prices in
hospitality and other labour-intensive sectors, base wages growth has been moderate for some years.
Business surveys point to non-labour cost escalation being the real issue lately. This is why we were
puzzled by the passage in the RBA minutes this week suggesting that cost pressures reported in
business surveys might imply that the unemployment rate consistent with inflation at target (the NAIRU)
has risen. It is a convenient belief, though, if unemployment is starting to overshoot your forecasts, as
the latest labour market data have done compared with the RBA’s August forecasts.

Not all firms can pass on cost increases. Most small firms do not have the market power, though coffee
may be an exception because convenience induces a kind of local market power. And as noted above,
large firms with everyday-low-price strategies will instead try to find savings elsewhere in the supply
chain.

Still, some firms will seek to pass these cost increases on. Some observers might see this as corporate
greed, but another way to frame this strategy is simply as a ‘cost-plus’ mentality. It turns out that cost-
plus pricing is especially common in areas where regulators determine the price – water and other
utilities being examples where costs and prices have escalated a lot in recent years.

There are limits to firms’ ability to absorb costs, too. The failures of so many building firms a few years
ago, squeezed between post-pandemic cost escalation, delivery delays and fixed-price contracts, were
a case in point. Normally, there is a contractual response, and it need not end in insolvency. The
episode does illustrate, however, that it is unrealistic to expect firms never to pass cost increases
through to prices.

The cost-plus mentality does have its drawbacks. It limits the imagination away from finding responses
that might save costs elsewhere to absorb the cost hike, and in so doing, boost efficiency. It was
equally limiting in times of soft demand: in the years before the pandemic, many firms professed a
belief that, because they couldn’t get price increases to stick, they could not possibly accept cost
increases, including in wages. Thus, Australia ended up with wages growth too low to be consistent
with inflation at target, and the peculiar spectacle of a central bank governor complaining that wages
growth was too low. And if labour remains cheap, why invest to economise on it and thereby boost
productivity?

A cost-plus mentality also amplifies the pass-through of supply shocks. This is not quite the same thing
as the ‘inflation expectations’ seen in standard economic models of inflation, but it is related. The
stronger the tendency towards cost-plus pricing, the worse the inflation response to a supply shock.
This is also true when inflation expectations are high, but the distinction matters. The recent academic
literature includes some fascinating empirical evidence from New Zealand that suggests that beliefs
about current and future overall inflation have little bearing on many firms’ pricing decisions. As related
work using French data shows, firms’ pricing choices are shaped less by economy-wide inflation
developments than by what is happening to their costs and in their industry.

### Page 4

Related articles
That adds up to economy-wide behaviour that looks a lot like firms (in aggregate) setting prices
according to expected inflation (in aggregate), but under the hood something quite different is going on.
If firms’ beliefs about future economy-wide inflation do not matter much in their pricing decisions, then
neither does the credibility of a central bank’s commitment to return inflation to target. The mechanism
to get inflation down then must be simply to weaken demand enough to convince firms that cost-plus
will not fly in the market. This is an unpleasant message for many consumers or anyone in an interest-
sensitive sector when supply shocks abound. It does, however, imply that if supply shocks fade or even
self-correct, so will high inflation. If and when that happens, tight monetary policy can be unwound.

The deeper question is whether cost-plus price dynamics can be reduced. That is more likely to be a
question for regulators than for central banks.

## Document 515: ロシア、財政運営はウクライナ戦争のさらなる長期化を鮮明に ～2027年度予算案はウクライナ戦争の長期化を前提、対日関係改善の道_bc450c02

Source PDF: all report pdf/ロシア、財政運営はウクライナ戦争のさらなる長期化を鮮明に ～2027年度予算案はウクライナ戦争の長期化を前提、対日関係改善の道_bc450c02.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

World Trends

2026 年 10 月 2 日

1/3 
ロシア、財政運営はウクライナ戦争のさらなる長期化を鮮明に 
～2027 年度予算案はウクライナ戦争の長期化を前提、対日関係改善の道筋も見えにくい～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ ロシア政府が提出した 2027 年度予算案では、歳出 48.8 兆ルーブルのうち国防費が 17.1 兆
ルーブルと全体の35％を占め、 今年度予算から４割以上増加するとしている。 安全保障機関
向けを含めれば関連支出は歳出全体の４割を上回り、プーチン政権がウクライナ戦争のさら
なる長期化を財政面で織り込んでいることがうかがえる。 
○ 一方、 戦争の長期化と国防費の膨張により財政状態は悪化している。2027 年の歳入は43.3 兆
ルーブルを見込み、原油・ガス収入の伸び悩みを、付加価値税や法人税などの税収増や追加
増税で補う方針である。財政赤字はＧＤＰ比▲2.2％と今年度の▲3.2％から縮小する想定と
しているが、歳出・債務を機動的に増やせる制度が整えられているため、さらなる財政悪化
の可能性は残る。公的債務残高も 2027 年末時点でＧＤＰ比 21.7％と、政府が従来「安全ラ
イン」としてきた20％を上回る見通しであり、戦費確保と財政健全性の両立が課題となる。 
○ こうしたなか、プーチン大統領は日本との協力に関心を示す一方、日本の「軍事化」やアジ
ア太平洋地域での安全保障枠組みに懸念を表明し、政治・安全保障面で日本をけん制した。
また、 ウクライナ戦争終結後の日ロ関係改善にも言及したものの、2027 年度予算案は戦争の
早期終結を前提としておらず、短期的には困難な状況にある。日ロ間では政治・安全保障上
の対立が続くため、経済関係だけを切り離して改善することは容易ではなく、日本にはロシ
ア側の具体的な政策・行動を見極めながら慎重に対応することが求められる。

【2027 年度予算で国防費は前年比４割超増、戦争継続と財政健全化の両立に難題】 
ロシア政府は９月 30 日、2027 年度予算案を連邦議会下院（国家院）に提出した。ウクライナ戦
争の開戦から４年半以上が経過するなか、ロシア経済にとっては軍事関連が一大産業となっている。
そのため、予算案における軍事費の動向は、ウクライナ戦争の先行きに加え、ロシア経済の行方を
大きく左右することが予想される。2027 年の歳出規模は 48.8 兆ルーブルとなり、そのうち国防費
は 17.1 兆ルーブルと歳出全体の35％を占める。国防費の規模は今年度予算（12.1 兆ルーブル）か
ら４割以上増加、従来計画（13.5 兆ルーブル）と比較しても３割弱膨らんだ。なお、国防費とは別
に連邦政府の安全保障機関（対外情報庁（ＳＶＲ）や連邦保安庁（ＦＳＢ）など）に関連予算が計

### Page 2

2/3 
上されており、 これらを合わせると歳出全体の４割以上にのぼる。 こうした予算配分を勘案すると、
プーチン政権がウクライナ戦争のさらなる長期化を前提としている様子がうかがえる。 
ウクライナ戦争の長期化やそれに伴う国防費の増大を受けて、財政状況は急速に悪化している。
予算案では、2027 年の歳入規模を 43.3 兆ルーブル、そのうち原油・ガス関連収入は７兆ルーブル
とした。前提となるウラル原油価格は１バレル＝53 ドルとしている。 足元の原油価格は高止まりし
ているものの、 今年度の原油・ガス関連収入を8.9 兆ルーブルから7.6 兆ルーブルに下方修正して
いる。背景には、原油価格やウラル原油のディスカウント、原油・ガスの生産・輸出動向など複数
の要因があるとみられる。一方で、足元ではインフレが加速するなか、付加価値税（ＶＡＴ）や物
品税、 輸入関税などの税収は今年度予算から１割程度の増加を見込むほか、 法人税も同様に増加す
るとの見方を示す。さらに、膨張する国防費を賄うべく、金融所得などを対象とする追加増税も検
討しており、財政悪化に歯止めをかけたいとの狙いもうかがえる。 
その結果、2027 年の財政赤字は 5.5 兆ルーブル、ＧＤＰ比で▲2.2％となる。足元の財政状況が
悪化していることを受けて、今年度の財政赤字はＧＤＰ比▲3.2％と当初案（同▲1.6％）から赤字
幅が大幅に引き上げられたものの、一転して来年度の赤字幅は縮小するとしている。ただし、今年
６月に連邦議会は予算法の改正を経ない形で歳出、債務の拡大を可能にする法律を可決しており、
国防費をはじめとする歳出を機動的に増加させることが可能となっている。政府によれば、2026 年
だけを対象とする特例措置としたものの、 先日実施された総選挙では与党が３分の２を超える議席
を獲得しており （注１） 、 先行きもなし崩し的に同様の対応が進む可能性には注意が必要である。公
的債務残高は 2027 年末時点でＧＤＰ比21.7％と国際的には低水準にとどまるものの、政府がこれ
まで「安全ライン」 とみなしてきた20％と上回る。 このため、 戦争継続に伴う歳出圧力と財政健全
性の維持をいかに両立させるか、ロシア政府にとって一段と重要な課題となろう。

【安全保障面で日本をけん制する一方、経済協力の余地は残したいロシア】 
プーチン大統領は１日、国内外の有識者が集まる国際会議（ワルダイ会議）に登壇し、日本との
関係について、協力に関心があるとする一方、日本の「軍事化」の動きに懸念を抱いていると述べ
たと報じられている。プーチン氏はこのところ、中国が国際場裡で日本を批判する際に用いる「新
型軍国主義」とのナラティブ（物語）に歩調をあわせる動きをみせており、今回の発言もそうした
文脈の延長線上にあると考えられる。また、アジア太平洋地域の安全保障環境を巡って、軍事・政
治的なブロック形成に懸念を抱いていると述べるなど、日米豪印戦略対話（Quad）や米英豪による
安全保障枠組み （AUKUS） を念頭に置いた可能性がある。 一方で、 ウクライナ戦争の終了後には日ロ
関係が改善するとの見通しを示し、 日本側に関係改善に向けた対応を求めた。 しかし、2027 年度予
算案の内容は、 早期にウクライナ戦争が終結する可能性が低いことを示唆しており、 現実的ではな
いと捉える必要があろう。プーチン氏の発言は、政治・安全保障面で日本をけん制する一方、経済

注１ ９月24 日付レポート「ロシア下院選、与党･統一ロシアが３分の２超の議席を維持」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/665980.html）

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
面では協力の余地を残したいとの意図がうかがえる。しかし、日ロ関係を巡る政治・安全保障上の
対立が続くなか、経済関係のみを切り分けて改善することには相応の難しさが伴う。このため、日
本にとってはロシア側の今後の具体的な政策や行動を見極めつつ、 慎重に対応する局面が続くと考
えられる。

景気は内需主因で停滞
◆消費・投資はコロナ禍以来の低い伸び
中国景気は内需の不振で足踏み 。小売売上高
は、財消費の低迷を主因に前年並みの水準で推移。
耐久消費財の買い替え補助金の効果がはく落して
いるほか、中東危機によるガソリン価格の高騰などを
背景に消費者マインドが一段と悪化 。固定資産投
資はインフラ投資などで底打ちの兆しがみられるもの
の、昨年以降大幅な減少が継続。
一方、外需は高い伸びを維持 。世界的なAIブー
ムを背景にIT関連財の輸出が引き続き好調である
ほか、EV需要の高まりを受けて自動車輸出台数も
3カ月連続で過去最高水準。もっとも、原油や半導
体などの価格上昇の影響も大きく 、実質ベースでは、
内需の減速を補うには至らず。
◆成長率目標の達成は困難
先行きの景気も、内需を中心に停滞が続く見通し。
ハイテク製造業の投資拡大や電力 ・通信網などの
「６つのネットワーク 」推進によるインフラ投資の持ち
直しが見込まれるものの 、不動産不況や過剰生産

能力といった構造的な下押し要因が残存し 、投資
は総じて低調に推移する見込み。悲観的な消費者
マインドが続くなか、消費も低迷が続く公算大。
一方、政府は景気刺激策に慎重 。成長率よりも
経済の質を重視し 、不動産市場や地方政府債務
などのリスク解消に注力する姿勢を明確にしており 、
2026年の成長率目標は達成困難な状況。
経済政策スタンス（鐘才文コラム）
（資料）人民網などを基に日本総研作成
（注）中央財経委員会が「鐘才文」名義で掲載した文章。
現状認識
・AI、ロボット、ハイテク製造業など新たな成長ドラ
イバーが台頭
・成長モデルの転換期にあり、成長率や月次指標
よりも経済運営の質を重視
・供給は強い一方、需要が弱い状況は残存
経済運営の
方向性
・AIや先端製造業を軸とした産業高度化を推進
・大規模な景気刺激策には依存しない
・不動産市場や地方政府債務などのリスク解消策
に注力（短期の成長率にはマイナスとなるも、長
期かつ持続的な経済発展にはプラス）
下半期の
経済見通し
・第15次５カ年計画などで確定した重点プロジェ

消費は総じて低調
◆サービス消費は堅調も、財消費が低迷
８月の小売売上高は前年比＋ 0.3％と、ほぼゼ
ロ成長。財消費の低迷が主因であり、なかでも自動
車が大幅減。買い替え補助政策で喚起された需要
の反動減に加え、原油高でガソリン車需要が減退。
宝飾品も 、国際的な金価格の上昇と節約志向の
高まりが相まって、販売数量が大幅減。
先行きの消費も、総じて低調に推移する見通し。
消費者マインドは中東危機を契機に悪化基調 。６
月に一時持ち直したものの 、ガソリン価格の再上昇
を受けて再び低下 。政府は利子補給制度の対象
拡大などで内需を喚起しているものの 、大規模な消
費刺激策は打ち出さない姿勢。一方、サービス消費
は今後の消費拡大の重点分野に位置づけられてお
り、堅調に推移する見込み。
◆住宅市場は長期停滞から脱却できず
住宅市場は引き続き低迷。大都市を中心に住宅
購入要件の緩和などが実施されているものの 、購入
層の減少といった構造的な要因が住宅販売の重石

に。新築住宅価格に加え 、市場の実勢を反映しや
すい中古住宅価格も引き続き下落。在庫の回転期
間も長期化している模様。住宅市場の健全化に向
け、予約販売から完成後販売への移行や住宅ロー
ン返済期間の延長などが打ち出されたものの 、効果
が短期間で現れるとは見込めず 、住宅市場の本格
回復にはなお数年を要する見通し。

◆投資は下げ止まりの兆し
８月の固定資産投資は前年比 ▲10.6％と、大
幅減が続いているものの、一部で底打ちの兆し。セク
ター別にみると 、インフラ投資は 、「６つのネットワー
ク」の推進などを背景にマイナス幅が縮小。一方、製
造業投資は減少幅が拡大 。政府は経済運営の質
を重視する姿勢をとっており、過剰生産能力の解消
が投資の重石になっている可能性。不動産投資も、
住宅市場の低迷を背景に大幅減少が継続。
◆年末にかけてマイナス幅が縮小
先行きの投資は、以下２点から減少幅が縮小す
る見通し。第１に、ハイテク製造業の投資拡大。政
府による支援も相まって、半導体やロボットなど先端
分野は増産基調。第２に、インフラ投資の持ち直し。
8,000億元の新型政策性金融ツールと「６つのネッ
トワーク（六張網）」が年後半のインフラ投資を押し
上げ。六張網は官民が重点的にインフラ整備を進め
る戦略分野であり 、既存整備計画の前倒しのみな
らず、次世代通信網や計算能力網などの新規計画

も立ち上げ。足元では、情報伝送や鉄道輸送 、航
空輸送に関連する固定資産投資に底打ちの兆し。
もっとも、本格回復は見込めない状況 。製造業や
不動産業では、過剰な生産能力や在庫などの構造
的な下押し要因が残存 。インフラ投資も量的拡大
は限定的。財源となる地方債は借り換えや隠れ債
務の圧縮が優先され、新発債の発行は停滞。
投資は下げ止まるも、弱い動き

### Page 6

中国
(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年9月5
外需は先行きも堅調
◆輸出入ともに価格上昇の影響大
８月の名目輸出は前年比＋ 24.9％と、高い伸
び。ASEAN・NIEs向けでは、IT関連財の輸出が引
き続き好調であるほか 、中東危機による原油供給
懸念を背景にEV輸出も拡大。加えて、対米輸出が
米中関係の改善を受けて堅調に推移しているほか、
EU向け輸出も、年初ほどの勢いはないものの底堅く
推移。こうした輸出額の増加には 、価格上昇の影
響も大。原油価格が高止まりしているほか 、AIブー
ムを背景に半導体や銅 、金属鉱石などの価格も上
昇。実質ベースの輸出の伸びは弱く、内需の減速を
補うには至らず。
先行きも、外需は堅調に推移する見通し。９月の
米中首脳会談でも貿易摩擦の緩和に向けた認識
で一致しており、対米輸出は前年比プラスで推移す
る見通し。また、中東危機を受けて世界的な脱炭
素化が一段と加速しており 、EV輸出が拡大する公
算大。ASEANなどへの中間財輸出も 、AIブームを

背景に好調を維持する見込み。
◆資金流出は拡大傾向
金融収支は流出傾向が継続 。内需の不振で対
内直接投資が減少しているほか 、準備資産も縮小。
商業銀行などへの外貨資金の振替・預託や資産再
配分が進行している可能性 。先行きも、内需の低
迷が対内投資の重石となり 、金融収支でみた資本
流出傾向が継続する見通し。

### Page 7

中国
(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年9月6
◆根強いディスインフレ圧力
８月の生産者物価（PPI）は前年比＋3.8％と、
高水準で推移。原油価格に起因する生産財価格
の高止まりが主因 。消費者物価（CPI）も、総合
ベースで同＋0.8％、コアベースで同＋1.0％と、前
月からやや加速。交通燃料のプラス幅が再び拡大し
たほか、半導体の価格高騰で通信機器が上昇 。
もっとも、インフレ率は政府目標の２ ％前後を大きく
下回っており、内需の停滞に起因するディスインフレ
圧力が強い状況。
先行きも、エネルギー価格の高止まりや半導体価
格の上昇を背景に一部財の価格上昇圧力は根強
いものの 、内需の低迷により基調的なインフレ率は
低位で推移する見込み。
◆対米ドルレートは増価基調
９月の人民元の対米ドルレートは増価基調で推
移。中東危機が経済に与える影響や金融政策の
動向を見極めようと、市場の様子見姿勢が続くもの
の、好調な輸出や資本流出の抑制に向けた人民銀
行の元高容認姿勢もあり、小幅な人民元高圧力が

続く見通し。
◆株価は下落基調
９月の上海総合株価指数は下落。PPIの上昇率
が一服したことを好感して持ち直す動きがみられるも
のの、中東情勢の不透明感や内需の弱さが相場の
重石に。景気が停滞局面に入る なか、先行きも弱
い動きが続く見込み。
インフレ率は低位で推移
